### `junit-team/junit-framework` — JVM/Java multi-module Gradle library (JUnit 5), community-governed, EPL-2.0

**Addressable replaceable today:** ~70% of addressable (non-execution) validation. The execution-dominated remainder (Roseau/OSGi/reproducibility/self-tests) is correctly category-D.

**Enforcement surface.** JUnit's bespoke validation is almost entirely Gradle-task-driven and runs as tool execution. License-headers: Spotless applies/verifies the EPL-2.0 Java header via licenseHeaderFile(gradle/config/spotless/eclipse-public-license-2.0.java) in junitbuild.spotless-conventions.gradle.kts; Spotless also formats (google-java-format / ktlint-style), import-orders, and trims whitespace. Structure/layout: settings.gradle.kts:99-104 sets project.buildFileName="${project.name}.gradle.kts" then require(buildFile.isFile) — every module dir X must hold X.gradle.kts (a hard build-time assertion). Naming/content hygiene: Checkstyle (checkstyleMain.xml) plus a RegexpSingleline that bans Javadoc @author; nohttp/checkstyleNohttp.xml forbids http:// (non-https) URLs across the tree; CONTRIBUTING bans @author. Cross-file coherence: gradle/libs.versions.toml is the version catalog (single source of truth for dependency versions); OSGi metadata and Roseau API-compatibility checks compare the built artifacts against a baseline. Codegen/reproducibility: a reproducible-build verification task; API signature (roseau) gate. Commit-discipline: .github/dco.yml enables the DCO GitHub App, requiring a Signed-off-by trailer on every commit. Supply-chain: gradle/wrapper/gradle-wrapper.properties pins distributionSha256Sum and validateDistributionUrl=true. Execution-only: the JUnit Platform self-test suite, Spotless formatting, Checkstyle AST rules, Roseau API diff, OSGi bnd analysis — all run via Gradle, not repo-state assertions.

**What alint replaces.** EPL-2.0 header invariant -> file_header (junit-epl-license-header); per-module <name>.gradle.kts naming/structure assertion (settings.gradle.kts require) -> for_each_dir + nested file_exists with {basename} (junit-module-has-named-build-file); DCO Signed-off-by trailer (.github/dco.yml) -> git_commit_signed_off (junit-dco-signed-off); no Javadoc @author (Checkstyle RegexpSingleline) -> file_content_forbidden (junit-no-author-tags); gradle-wrapper SHA-256 pin + validateDistributionUrl -> file_content_matches (junit-gradle-wrapper-sha-pinned / -validate-url); required build/quality config files present -> file_exists (junit-build-config-files-exist); NOTICE/KEYS/SECURITY governance docs -> file_exists.

**What alint improves.** Consolidates four otherwise-separate mechanisms (Spotless header task, a settings.gradle.kts require(), the DCO GitHub App, and a Checkstyle regex rule) into one declarative, reviewable file that runs with zero JVM/Gradle bootstrap — JUnit's checks today need a full Gradle daemon + plugin resolution. The for_each_dir + {basename} rule expresses the module<->build-file naming edge as data, whereas the repo buries it in Kotlin DSL that only fires when Gradle configures the project; alint catches a renamed-but-not-updated module before configuration. The nohttp invariant (forbid http:// URLs) is a clean file_content_forbidden over the whole tree — strictly lower-overhead than nohttp's Checkstyle integration. file_header anchors on the license body (not the varying $YEAR), giving fewer false-positives than a literal-string compare.

**Genuine gaps (post-build, critically assessed).** none material. The Roseau API-compatibility diff, OSGi bnd metadata validation, reproducible-build byte-stability check, and the version-catalog-resolves-correctly behavior are all execution/semantic gates (category D) — correctly out of scope. One borderline item: the nohttp allowlist (some http:// URLs are legitimately exempted via a checkstyle suppression file) — alint's file_content_forbidden has paths.exclude but cannot exempt a specific *line/URL* within an otherwise-scanned file; the repo's suppression granularity is finer. That is a known, narrow scoping limit, not a missing capability — for whole-file exclusion alint is fine.

**Abstraction ideas.** A bundled 'gradle-supply-chain' micro-ruleset (wrapper SHA-256 pin + validateDistributionUrl + libs.versions.toml present) would package the three wrapper/catalog assertions every Gradle repo wants. A first-class 'nohttp' shared option (forbid non-https URLs with an inline allowlist of permitted hosts) would generalize a pattern seen across JVM/Spring-adjacent repos; the inline-line-allowlist is the genuinely new primitive (today file_content_forbidden is whole-file/whole-match). Otherwise the existing surface covers JUnit well.

*Notes.* Config is accurate and well-scoped; the A1/A2/A3 classification holds under the current brief. DCO rule is HEAD-only on a depth-1 study clone — meaningful only with since:/full history. No stale gap claims in this config.

### `kubernetes/kubernetes` — Go CNCF hyperscale monorepo (root go.mod + go.work over ~40 staging/src/k8s.io/* published modules), Apache-2.0, Prow CI

**Addressable replaceable today:** ~55-60% of ADDRESSABLE (non-execution) validation. The repo's enforcement is heavily codegen-freshness + import-boss layering + tests (largely category D, orchestratable via command/command_idempotent but not reimplemented); of the pure repo-state checks, alint replaces the large majority, leaving the two genuine gaps above.

**Enforcement surface.** Enforcement is dominated by ~50 hack/verify-*.sh scripts driven by `make verify` on Prow (no .github/workflows/). License-headers: hack/verify-boilerplate.sh + boilerplate.py compares every .go/.sh/.py against hack/boilerplate/boilerplate.<ext>.txt, normalizing the Copyright YYYY year and skipping generated/vendored/third_party paths. Structure/layout: hack/verify-staging-meta-files.sh requires a fixed governance-file set (OWNERS, LICENSE, SECURITY_CONTACTS, code-of-conduct.md, .github/PULL_REQUEST_TEMPLATE.md, README.md) in each staging/src/k8s.io/<repo> (client-go README is the documented exception). Import-firewalls: hack/verify-prometheus-imports.sh greps for github.com/prometheus/* imports and fails unless the file is in allowed_prometheus_importers, AND separately asserts that allowlist must only SHRINK (a snapshot count must-not-grow check); hack/verify-import-boss.sh runs import-boss for layered import rules across packages. Security/CVE firewalls: hack/verify-netparse-cve.sh forbids stdlib net.ParseIP/net.ParseCIDR (golang 1.17 leading-zero compat). Hygiene/size: hack/verify-file-sizes.sh caps only BINARY files >1 MiB (uses `git ls-files --eol`, checks only w/-text=binary entries; large generated TEXT like swagger.json/*.pb.go/zz_generated.openapi.go is deliberately allowed). Codegen-freshness (the BIG bucket): hack/verify-codegen.sh, verify-generated-*.sh, verify-vendor.sh, verify-mocks.sh — regenerate then `git diff --exit-code`. Toolchain: .go-version pins the Go version consumed by hack/lib/golang.sh; build/dependencies.yaml is the zeitgeist external-version policy. Execution-only: gofmt/goimports, golangci-lint, go vet, the import-boss layering engine, conformance/unit/integration tests, the OpenAPI/protobuf generators — all category D.

**What alint replaces.** Apache-2.0 boilerplate header on .go and .sh (verify-boilerplate.sh) -> file_header x2 (k8s-go-boilerplate-header / k8s-shell-boilerplate-header); staging governance-file set (verify-staging-meta-files.sh) -> for_each_dir + nested file_exists (k8s-staging-meta-files / k8s-staging-readme); prometheus import firewall (verify-prometheus-imports.sh, forward arm) -> import_gate language:go + paths.exclude allowlist (k8s-prometheus-import-firewall); stdlib net.ParseIP/ParseCIDR ban (verify-netparse-cve.sh) -> file_content_forbidden x2; .go-version shape -> file_content_matches; root governance docs / go.work / build/dependencies.yaml present -> file_exists.

**What alint improves.** Collapses ~6 standalone bash verify scripts (each its own grep + allowlist-array + exit-code dance, individually CI-wired) into one schema-validated, reviewable config that needs no `make` / Go toolchain to run. The boilerplate header is a single file_header with a precise exclude-set vs boilerplate.py's bespoke .generated_files parsing. import_gate (go preset) extracts the actual import target and matches forbid '^github.com/prometheus/' — semantically cleaner and lower-false-positive than the script's `grep -RlEe '"github.com/prometheus/.*"'`, which can match the path inside comments/strings. The netparse CVE ban becomes two declarative rules instead of a maintained grep script. Cross-file: .go-version<->go.mod `go` directive coherence (config defers this to 'the Go toolchain enforces it = D', under-claiming) is in fact expressible — cross_file relation equals, source .go-version regex '^(\d+\.\d+)' normalize semver-minor, target go.mod regex 'go (\d+\.\d+)' — alint catches a drifted .go-version that the toolchain only notices at build time.

**Genuine gaps (post-build, critically assessed).** Two real ones, both tied to k8s's bespoke semantics. (1) The hack/verify-file-sizes.sh BINARY-ONLY size cap: alint's file_max_size has no text/binary discriminator — confirmed FileEntry exposes only path/is_dir/size (walker.rs:46-50), no `git ls-files --eol` w/-text attribute — so a naive `**` over-fires on the large generated TEXT files k8s intentionally permits (swagger.json/*.pb.go). I tried file_max_size with broad globs (over-fires) and the config's mitigation (enumerate binary extensions) which is honest but loses the 'any committed binary' generality and can't see an extension-less binary; this is a true sharp-edge (wants a text-aware/binary-only scope). (2) The prometheus 'allowlist must only SHRINK' reverse check — a stateful snapshot-count-must-not-grow assertion. I tried registry_paths_resolve orphans (reverse-completeness exists, but it asserts 'on-disk artefact not referenced' not 'allowlist count vs a frozen baseline') and file_graph no_orphans (same: graph-reachability, not a count ratchet); neither encodes 'this set may shrink but never grow relative to a committed baseline'. alint has no baseline/ratchet primitive. Note: the forward prometheus arm IS replaced; only the ratchet half is the gap. The codegen-freshness scripts (verify-codegen/generated/vendor/mocks) are NOT gaps — generated_file_fresh (outputs: mutating mode) and command_idempotent cover the 'regen then git diff' pattern; they're category D only in the sense that the user supplies the generator command.

**Abstraction ideas.** (1) A 'binary-only' / text-aware scope axis (e.g. file_max_size with content_class: binary, or a shared `only: binary|text` selector that consults a content sniff or .gitattributes eol) — would natively express verify-file-sizes.sh and is a recurring monorepo need. (2) A baseline/ratchet primitive: a rule wrapper that records a committed snapshot (count, or set of paths) and fails when the live value GROWS (allowlist-must-shrink, deprecated-import-count-must-not-increase) — k8s's prometheus reverse check, and the same 'budget' pattern appears in many large repos. (3) An import_gate 'allowlist completeness' mode (forbid the import everywhere AND flag any allowlist entry that no longer imports it) to keep the exclude-set honest. These are genuine, grounded extensions, not speculative.

*Notes.* Config classification is sound; the C1 file-size sharp-edge is correctly identified. Under-claim found: A6 .go-version coherence is IMPROVABLE (cross_file), not category-D. The 'allowlist must shrink' C-note is a true ratchet gap worth a backlog item.

---

### `laravel/framework` — PHP web-framework library, Composer monorepo splitsh-lite-split into ~35 read-only illuminate/* subtree repos, MIT

**Addressable replaceable today:** ~85% of ADDRESSABLE (non-execution) validation — once the boolean json_path_equals is reinstated, essentially all of laravel's registry/coherence surface is alint-native. The large execution remainder (PHPUnit/PHPStan/StyleCI) is correctly category-D.

**Enforcement surface.** What laravel/framework actively gates is dominated by execution: PHPUnit across a php/phpunit/stability/DB/queue/redis CI matrix, and PHPStan (larastan) over src + a types/ stub tree — both deliberate category-D. Cross-file/registry coherence (the addressable surface): composer.json autoload.files[*] (9 global helper files) and autoload-dev.files[*] must exist or Composer fatals at autoload time (a Composer-enforced invariant); autoload.psr-4 namespace roots must be real directories; bin/split.sh enumerates `split 'src/Illuminate/<Component>'` for each of ~35 components and the splitsh-lite release run breaks if any prefixed dir is missing. Hygiene: a StyleCI config (php-cs-fixer ruleset, PSR-12-ish) formats the tree — runs as a hosted service, execution. Version coherence: the root composer.json extra.branch-alias.dev-master is hand-synced across every per-component composer.json on each major; nothing in CI checks it. Structure: README.md + LICENSE.md present. Execution-only: php-cs-fixer/StyleCI formatting, PHPStan static analysis, PHPUnit suites — category D.

**What alint replaces.** composer.json autoload.files[*] existence (Composer autoload-time invariant) -> registry_paths_resolve extract json $.autoload.files[*] expect:file (laravel-composer-autoload-files-resolve); autoload-dev.files[*] -> registry_paths_resolve (laravel-composer-autoload-dev-files-resolve); bin/split.sh component dirs -> registry_paths_resolve extract regex expect:dir (laravel-split-component-dirs-resolve); autoload.psr-4 namespace roots -> registry_paths_resolve expect:dir (laravel-composer-psr4-dirs-resolve); branch-alias coherence across component composer.json files -> cross_file_value_equals / cross_file relation:equals (laravel-branch-alias-coherent); README.md+LICENSE.md -> oss-baseline.

**What alint improves.** registry_paths_resolve turns the implicit 'Composer fatals at autoload time if a helper file is missing' contract into an EXPLICIT, pre-merge declarative check — caught before any PHP process runs, no Composer install needed. The bin/split.sh assertion (every split path is a real dir) is something the repo only discovers at RELEASE time when splitsh-lite fails; alint surfaces a renamed-but-not-updated component immediately, and expresses it as a regex-extracted registry over the actual shell script. The branch-alias coherence (cross_file relation:equals over src/Illuminate/*/composer.json) is a real cross-file invariant the repo hand-maintains with NO gate and NO documented checklist — pure alint value-add that no current laravel check covers. registry_paths_resolve also offers an orphans reverse-check (a src/Illuminate/* dir with no matching split entry) the bin/split.sh approach lacks.

**Genuine gaps (post-build, critically assessed).** none. CRITICAL CORRECTION to the config's own note: it claims a 'config.sort-packages == true' assertion was REMOVED because 'the structured-path family drops non-string JSON nodes, so it cannot assert a boolean/numeric value (would emit a spurious path-not-found)'. That is now STALE/FIXED — verified structured_path.rs check_match Op::Equals does `m == expected` on the full serde_json::Value (lines 419-428), comparing bools/numbers/null natively; json_path_equals with equals: true correctly asserts config.sort-packages. Only the `matches` op is string-only (line 431). So this is a REPLACEABLE check the config wrongly recorded as a tuning gap. The PHPUnit/PHPStan/php-cs-fixer gates are execution (category D, correctly out of scope), not gaps.

**Abstraction ideas.** A bundled 'php'/'composer' ruleset (composer.json autoload.files/psr-4 resolve + autoload-dev.files resolve + branch-alias coherence + composer.json schema sanity) would package what every Composer monorepo needs and slot beside the existing python/go/rust/node/dotnet bundles — laravel is the canonical demonstrator. A 'subtree-split manifest' shared idiom (a release script enumerates component prefixes; assert each resolves AND each on-disk component is enumerated) generalizes bin/split.sh + the orphan reverse-check into one named pattern. Nothing beyond the existing surface is actually missing.

*Notes.* Config under-reports: the removed sort-packages boolean assertion is now expressible (stale gap from a PRE-build authoring). Otherwise A1-A4 + V1 are accurate and the registry_paths_resolve modeling of the splitsh-lite contract is a strong fit.

### `llvm/llvm-project` — C++ compiler-infrastructure monorepo (~25 subprojects: llvm/clang/mlir/lld/lldb/flang/libcxx/compiler-rt/...), CMake, Apache-2.0-WITH-LLVM-exception

**Addressable replaceable today:** ~40-50% of ADDRESSABLE (non-execution) validation. LLVM is execution-dominated (clang-format/tidy/buildifier + CMake/lit/sphinx/pytest = category D), so the addressable slice is small; within it alint replaces nearly everything (header, ascii/tabs, hash-lock, version-file shape, CODEOWNERS), leaving only the git-describe version-coherence gap.

**Enforcement surface.** LLVM's enforced surface is almost entirely GitHub-Actions execution gates plus reviewer policy. License-headers: the canonical LLVM source banner ('Part of the LLVM Project ... SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception') is mandated by DeveloperPolicy.html#license and enforced by human reviewers (NO automated gate) on every hand-written source file. Formatting/lint (execution): pr-code-format runs clang-format --diff on changed files (hard gate); pr-code-lint runs clang-tidy on clang-tools-extra changes; bazel-checks runs buildifier --mode=check + bazelisk test. Build/test (execution): premerge.yaml does CMake build + lit/unit suites on Linux/Win/ARM. Codegen-freshness: libcxx-check-generated runs `ninja libcxx-generate-files && git diff --exit-code`, AND in the same run-buildbot check-generated-output does `! grep -rn '[^ -~]' libcxx/include libcxx/src libcxx/test` — rejecting BOTH non-ASCII bytes and hard tabs in libcxx. Version coherence: version-check (release/** only) asserts cmake/Modules/LLVMVersion.cmake == `git describe` tag. Commit/author: email-check rejects a PR author *.noreply.github.com email. Supply-chain: scorecard/zizmor (OSSF + GHA static analysis); pip --require-hashes installs a hash-locked .ci/all_requirements.txt. Docs: docs.yml sphinx build. CI self-test: check-ci runs pytest over .ci/*_test.py. Formatting config: root .clang-format selects BasedOnStyle: LLVM; pyproject.toml [tool.black] excludes third-party/. Execution-only: clang-format/clang-tidy/buildifier, the entire CMake+lit build, sphinx, pytest, scorecard — all category D.

**What alint replaces.** LLVM canonical source-file header / SPDX banner (DeveloperPolicy reviewer-enforced) -> file_header (llvm-cxx-license-header); libcxx no-hard-tabs arm of check-generated-output -> file_content_forbidden pattern '\t' (llvm-libcxx-no-hard-tabs); root pyproject.toml present -> file_exists; LLVMVersion.cmake present + declares LLVM_VERSION_MAJOR -> file_exists + file_content_matches; .github/CODEOWNERS present -> file_exists; .ci/all_requirements.txt hash-locked (--hash=sha256:) -> file_content_matches; .clang-format BasedOnStyle: LLVM -> file_content_matches.

**What alint improves.** The single biggest LLVM invariant — the source-file license header — is enforced ONLY by human reviewers today (no CI gate); file_header turns it into an automatable, pre-review check across llvm/clang/mlir/lld, anchored on the stable SPDX line with a precise exclude-set (*.inc TableGen output, test/unittests/benchmarks, third-party). That is a strict upgrade over reviewer eyeballing. The libcxx non-ASCII/no-tabs grep is replaced more expressively: the hard-tab arm is file_content_forbidden '\t', and the non-ASCII arm maps cleanly onto file_is_ascii (with allow: for any sanctioned codepoints) — together a declarative pair vs a bespoke `grep -rn '[^ -~]'`. The hash-locked-requirements and clang-format-style assertions become reviewable config instead of being implicit in CI yaml. IMPROVABLE upgrade the config under-claims: the version-check (LLVMVersion.cmake == git tag, currently a release/**-only GHA) can be partly expressed — cross_file/file_content cannot read `git describe`, but git_commit / git tag context plus a content assertion narrows it; at minimum the LLVMVersion.cmake shape is alint-owned (the config does this).

**Genuine gaps (post-build, critically assessed).** Mostly none — but list honestly. (1) The libcxx non-ASCII arm: the config only encodes the hard-tab half with file_content_forbidden '\t'. The FULL `grep '[^ -~]'` (any byte outside 0x20-0x7e) is better served by file_is_ascii (mentioned in the brief with allow: codepoints) — I'd reclassify that as REPLACEABLE, not a gap; the config simply under-used the kind. (2) version-check tying LLVMVersion.cmake to `git describe`: alint cannot read the git-describe tag string to compare against a file value (no extractor pulls `git describe` output into cross_file). I tried cross_file (no git-tag source) and git_commit_* (asserts commit metadata, not 'file value == nearest tag'); genuinely not expressible — but it's a release-only gate and arguably category-D-adjacent (semantic version arithmetic). (3) clang-format/clang-tidy/buildifier formatting and the CMake+lit build/test are execution (category D), correctly out of scope; codegen-freshness (libcxx-generate-files then git diff) IS expressible via generated_file_fresh (outputs: mutating mode) — not a gap, just user-command-supplied.

**Abstraction ideas.** A first-class git-tag/describe extractor for cross_file (source: { git: describe } or { git: latest_tag }) so 'a version file must equal the release tag' (LLVM version-check, and the same pattern in many release-gated repos) becomes native — this is the one genuinely missing primitive LLVM demonstrates. A bundled 'cpp'/'llvm-style' micro-ruleset (SPDX-WITH-exception header + file_is_ascii + no-hard-tabs + .clang-format style assertion) would package the C++-compiler-project conventions. Also: an 'SPDX-License-Identifier' shared header option (a parametrized license-banner rule keyed by SPDX id) would generalize the EPL/Apache/LLVM-exception header pattern recurring across this whole corpus.

*Notes.* Config is honest and correctly D-heavy. Two under-claims: the libcxx non-ASCII arm is REPLACEABLE via file_is_ascii (config only did the tab half), and codegen-freshness is expressible via generated_file_fresh. The git-describe<->LLVMVersion.cmake comparison is the one real missing primitive (suggests a git-tag extractor for cross_file).

### `mastodon-mastodon` — Ruby-on-Rails 8 monolith (Bundler) + JS/TS front-end + Node streaming server (yarn 4 Berry single-root workspace); AGPLv3; community governance

**Addressable replaceable today:** ~80% of the ADDRESSABLE (non-execution) surface; the enforced denominator is small and dominated by D-tools. Both previously-disabled content/structured rules are now expressible, raising effective coverage materially above the config's stated number.

**Enforcement surface.** Almost the entire ENFORCED surface is tool-execution in .github/workflows/* (category D): oxfmt format:check, stylelint, eslint+tsc, rubocop+brakeman, haml-lint, vitest, bundler-audit, codeql, the rails test/migration matrices, chromatic; plus .husky/pre-commit -> lint-staged running the same tools incrementally. The bespoke addressable surface is the i18n machinery: check-i18n.yml + bin/i18n-tasks + bin/rake repo:check_locales_files (locale-file parity across config/locales/*.yml, unused/missing strings, en.json formatjs codegen freshness, normalization). license-headers: NONE per-file (single root LICENSE = AGPL). structure/layout: implicit (Rails conventions, not gated). cross-file coherence (the addressable core): Ruby version must agree across .ruby-version (source of truth) / Gemfile.lock `RUBY VERSION` / Dockerfile `ARG RUBY_VERSION`; Node MAJOR must agree across .nvmrc / Dockerfile / streaming/Dockerfile — renovate.json5 documents both as manual-approval sync invariants. codegen-freshness: en.json regenerated from source strings (i18n-tasks). hygiene: README/LICENSE/SECURITY/CoC/.gitignore, lockfile presence, no tracked node_modules/dist, .editorconfig+.gitattributes eol. Two bespoke linters (lib/linter/rubocop_middle_dot.rb, haml_middle_dot.rb) wired into rubocop/haml-lint. Custom rubocop MiddleDot cop = a typographic AST check (D).

**What alint replaces.** Ruby-version tri-file coherence -> cross_file_value_equals (alias of cross_file relation:equals); Node-MAJOR coherence across .nvmrc + 2 Dockerfiles -> cross_file_value_equals with normalize semver-major; README/LICENSE/SECURITY/CoC/.gitignore + text hygiene -> oss-baseline@v1; lockfile present / no tracked node_modules|dist / engines / .nvmrc -> node@v1; .editorconfig+.gitattributes eol -> tooling/editorconfig@v1; no nested/stray lockfiles -> hygiene/lockfiles@v1; yarn-workspace members declare name/README -> monorepo/yarn-workspace@v1; workflow permissions/name/pinning -> ci/github-actions@v1; presence of the bespoke linters/locale sources/i18n-tasks/crowdin configs -> file_exists.

**What alint improves.** Consolidates the two hand-sync version invariants that today live only as PROSE in renovate.json5 comments ('needs to be synced with .ruby-version') into two declarative, schema-validated, reviewable cross_file rules that actually FAIL on drift — the repo has no executable gate for either. One `alint check` orchestrates the whole D-tool fleet (rubocop/eslint/stylelint/haml-lint/bundler-audit) as a single reviewable entrypoint atop fragmented CI jobs. en.json codegen freshness is now expressible (was 'i18n-tasks only'): generated_file_fresh mutating `outputs:` mode runs the formatjs extractor and diffs in place (snapshot->run->diff->restore), matching the CI git-diff check without re-implementing i18n-tasks.

**Genuine gaps (post-build, critically assessed).** none. CRITICAL REPRODUCE NOTE — both disabled rules in this config are PHANTOM gaps on current alint: (1) `mastodon-license-is-agpl` was disabled claiming `paths:['LICENSE']` silently no-ops for file_content_matches — FALSE: file_content_matches.rs tests (evaluate_passes_when_pattern_matches / evaluate_fires_when_pattern_missing, lines 170-199) prove a bare root literal `LICENSE` matches exactly; the real `**/LICENSE`-hits-vendored-Apache-files problem is solved by using the bare literal, not a wildcard. (2) `mastodon-publiccode-license` (yaml_path_equals $.legal.license on publiccode.yml) was disabled claiming `publiccode.yml` matches nothing — FALSE: structured_path.rs has a literal-path fast path (lines 321-344, `contains_file`), and its own tests use bare `action.yml`/`package.json`; `publiccode.yml` resolves. Both should be RE-ENABLED. The custom rubocop MiddleDot typographic AST cop and i18n unused/missing-string analysis are correct category-D non-goals (orchestrate, don't reimplement).

**Abstraction ideas.** A `multi_version_coherence` sugar / bundled fragment: many polyglot repos repeat the same shape (one source-of-truth version file + N Dockerfile/CI pins, MAJOR-only vs full). A 'renovate-sync-pact' ruleset that, given a source file + a list of {file, regex}, emits one cross_file per pin would compress this repo's two rules and recur across nearly every Docker+lang-manager monorepo. Lower priority: a `locale_parity` bundle (every locale file under a dir has the same key-set as the base) — partly cross_file set_equals per file via for_each_file, but a first-class 'all siblings share base key-set' would be cleaner.

*Notes.* The config's whole 'root-file scoping' lament (repeated in vscode + neovim too) is stale PRE-build analysis. ignore: of public/assets|packs dangling build-symlinks is a genuinely good, correct config decision worth keeping.

### `microsoft-TypeScript` — Large TS compiler/toolchain monorepo (npm, hereby task runner) — pinned at the LAST JS-based release (6.0); repo in MAINTENANCE MODE (future dev = microsoft/typescript-go)

**Addressable replaceable today:** ~75% of the ADDRESSABLE surface (the enforced addressable set is tiny: one version pin + one manifest + hygiene; the rest is D execution). With the JSONC fix the manifest rule now works, so coverage is higher than the config's stated number.

**Enforcement surface.** license-headers: src/*.ts carry NO per-file header (R6 absent). cross-file coherence (the one true enforced addressable invariant): .dprint.jsonc embeds `typescript-X.Y.Z.wasm` in the plugin URL and must equal the `@dprint/typescript` devDependency in package.json (in-repo comment: 'if updating typescript, update the one in package.json'). codegen/sync: src/lib/dom.generated.d.ts + webworker.generated.d.ts are SYNCED from microsoft/TypeScript-DOM-lib-generator and pr-modified-files.yml auto-CLOSES PRs that hand-edit them (a PR-diff+bot policy, D). registry: src/lib/libs.json (JSONC) enumerates 108 lib entries that Herebyfile generateLibs concatenates -> each must resolve to src/lib/<entry>.d.ts. execution gates (all D): dprint check, eslint --max-warnings 0 incl. 9 custom TSESTree AST rules under scripts/eslint/rules/, knip dead-export module-graph, runtests/runtests-parallel + the tests/baselines/reference accept loop (hereby baseline-accept then git diff --staged --exit-code), self-check (build/clean/rebuild --built), typecheck, smoke (npm pack + dynamic import probe), browser-integration (playwright), package-size (npm pack diff vs base), coverage (c8+codecov), codeql, scorecard. NOT-in-CI manual diagnostic tools: scripts/errorCheck.mjs + find-unused-diagnostic-messages.mjs pair diagnosticMessages.json KEYS against regex-counted occurrences across baselines/source by content. AGENTS.md carries a load-bearing 'This Repository Is in Maintenance Mode' marker that gates PR triage.

**What alint replaces.** dprint-plugin<->devDependency version agreement -> cross_file_value_equals (extract json on package.json + regex on the .wasm URL); libs.json -> <entry>.d.ts resolution -> registry_paths_resolve (extract json $.libs[*], base src/lib, entries_are_globs, expect file); README/LICENSE/SECURITY/.gitignore + hygiene -> oss-baseline@v1; lockfile/no-tracked-artifacts -> node@v1 + hygiene/no-tracked-artifacts@v1; AGENTS.md presence/structure -> agent-context@v1; .editorconfig+eol -> tooling/editorconfig@v1; .gitattributes `* -text` binary-default, AGENTS.md maintenance marker, ThirdPartyNoticeText.txt presence, package.json script wiring ($.scripts.lint == `hereby lint`, format contains `dprint fmt`, knip == `hereby knip`) -> file_content_matches / json_path_matches; generated DOM lib presence -> file_exists; dprint/eslint/knip orchestration -> command_idempotent (wrap only, the analysis stays the tool's).

**What alint improves.** The single enforced cross-file invariant (dprint plugin version) is today only a CODE COMMENT ritual — alint turns it into a failing, schema-validated check. registry_paths_resolve gives reverse-orphan detection over libs.json that generateLibs itself does not (a stale d.ts removed from disk but left in the manifest, or vice versa). command_idempotent wraps dprint/eslint/knip into one reviewable entrypoint over scattered CI jobs. The whole point is single-file declarative review of the version pin + manifest integrity that otherwise lives across a comment, a Herebyfile, and three workflows.

**Genuine gaps (post-build, critically assessed).** One narrow new-kind candidate, and it is NOT even CI-enforced: `registry_value_used` — assert each diagnosticMessages.json KEY is referenced >=1x somewhere in a content corpus (baselines/source) by regex. registry_paths_resolve resolves keys to FILE PATHS on disk; there is no 'key appears as a token in a text corpus' relation, and cross_file set_equals compares extracted value SETS not free-text occurrence. So scripts/find-unused-diagnostic-messages.mjs is genuinely unexpressible — but it is a manual diagnostic tool, not wired into CI, so it is value-add-only, not an enforced gap. CRITICAL REPRODUCE NOTE: the config's stated C-item ('libs.json is JSONC so registry_paths_resolve ERRORS on // comments') is RESOLVED on current alint — registry extracts via extract.rs::structured -> Format::Json.parse, which is JSONC-tolerant (strips // and /* */ and trailing commas; structured_path.rs:103-149, test json_parse_tolerates_jsonc). ts-libs-manifest-sources-resolve now PASSES; reclassify C->REPLACEABLE. The eslint custom AST rules / knip module-graph / baseline-accept compiler tests are correct category-D non-goals.

**Abstraction ideas.** `registry_value_used` (a.k.a. token_used): given a key-source (json/yaml/toml/regex extract) and a content corpus (glob set), every extracted key must occur >=1x (and optionally <=N) as a regex match across the corpus — the unused/duplicated-key family (TS diagnostics, gettext .pot, error-code tables). This is the natural inverse of registry_paths_resolve (resolves to files) and a counting-aware sibling of cross_file set_equals; it ALSO covers neovim B2's set-half. Build it once and two repos in this group light up. Secondary: a 'synced-from-upstream, do-not-hand-edit' marker is really a PR-diff policy (DOM libs) — alint could approximate with changeset_requires_path-style 'these paths must NOT be in the diff unless ack' but that is a role/bot policy and stays D.

*Notes.* Maintenance-mode repo: most 'enforcement' is the compiler build/test loop = irreducibly D. The honest alint value here is the version-pin pact + manifest integrity + single-entrypoint orchestration, all of which are real.

### `microsoft-vscode` — Very large TS editor/app monorepo (npm + gulp; historically yarn); Microsoft single-vendor; multi-target (electron/browser/remote)

**Addressable replaceable today:** ~70% of the ADDRESSABLE (tree-state) surface; the firewalls/formatters/tests (the bulk of vscode's enforcement effort) are correctly D. The 4 declared cross-file/header invariants are all natively expressible.

**Enforcement surface.** The bespoke engine is build/hygiene.ts (also the precommit hook via npm run precommit), with distinct behaviors: copyright header on TS/JS (first 4 lines must match the MS banner, scoped via build/filters.ts copyrightFilter excluding *.json/*.md/*.sh + copilot); product.json must NOT carry `extensionsGallery` (OSS build mustn't ship the MS Marketplace endpoint); no-unexpected-unicode with a curated allowlist; tab indentation (.editorconfig indent_style=tab, heavily exempted in filters.ts); TS formatting (tsfmt, D) / eslint (D) / stylelint (D); checkCopilotEnginesVersion: extensions/copilot/package.json engines.vscode === ^{root version}. build/gulpfile.hygiene.ts checkPackageJSON: any dep ALSO present in root must carry the IDENTICAL version in remote/, remote/web/, build/ package.json. import-firewalls/AST (all D via the TS type checker): build/checker/layersChecker.ts + tsconfig.<layer>.json (valid-layers-check) layer firewall; build/lib/checkCyclicDependencies.ts import-cycle detection over compiled out; build/lib/propertyInitOrderChecker.ts (define-class-fields-check); src/tsconfig.tsec.json tsec Trusted-Types security-typed compile. codegen-freshness: build/azure-pipelines/dependencies-check.yml -> check-clean-git-state.sh — after compiling /build, `git status --porcelain` must be empty (committed build/**/*.js are fresh vs .ts). commit/role policy (D): api-proposal-version-check.yml (bumping `// version: N` in a proposed-API d.ts needs a maintainer slash-command ack); no-engineering-system-changes.yml (PRs may not touch .github/workflows|build|package.json unless author privileged / sanctioned bot field-bump). telemetry.yml (vscode-telemetry-extractor, D); monaco-editor.yml (build, D); 10-OS x {electron,browser,remote} test matrix (D).

**What alint replaces.** MS copyright header on TS/JS -> file_header (lines:4 + pattern, include/exclude globs); product.json forbidding `extensionsGallery` -> file_content_forbidden (affirmative-absence, since json_path if_present no-ops on a missing key — correct choice); copilot engines.vscode == ^{root version} -> cross_file_value_equals (json $.version vs regex on the caret pin); a shared-dep version pin across remote/package.json -> cross_file_value_equals (spot-check); tab indentation in core src TS -> indent_style style:tabs; README/LICENSE/SECURITY/hygiene -> oss-baseline@v1; lockfile/no-tracked-artifacts -> node@v1; .editorconfig/.nvmrc/ThirdPartyNotices presence -> file_exists root_only.

**What alint improves.** Materially better on copyright + version pins: file_header is one declarative reviewable rule vs the hand-rolled hygiene.ts copyrights/filters pair, and it runs without `npm ci`/the TS build (hygiene.ts can't gate a bare clone). The copilot-engines and remote-dep version pacts become schema-validated rules instead of bespoke gulp logic. file_content_forbidden affirmatively forbids extensionsGallery (a positive presence check the json-path family can't do when the key is simply absent) — a real lower-false-negative win. CRITICAL: the codegen-freshness check (check-clean-git-state.sh: build/**/*.js fresh vs .ts) — NOT in the original config — is now expressible via generated_file_fresh mutating `outputs:` mode (run the /build compile, diff build/**/*.js in place, restore), giving a clean-tree assertion without the azure-pipelines plumbing.

**Genuine gaps (post-build, critically assessed).** none that vscode actually GATES that alint should do. The config's C2 ('checkPackageJSON: EVERY dep shared with root must match across remote/remote-web/build, full map-intersection') is the closest candidate — cross_file_value_equals only does one named key per rule, so 'every shared key' would need one rule per dep. But REPRODUCE-FIRST: this is enumerable (the shared set is known and stable; one cross_file per shared dep, or a generated rule block) and cross_file already does set relations on extracted sets — a true map-INTERSECTION-equality variant would be sugar, not a capability the repo's check needs that alint fundamentally lacks. I tried framing it as cross_file set_equals on $.dependencies keys (proves the key-SETS match) which catches drift in WHICH deps exist but not per-key VERSION equality; that residual is the only soft edge, and it is enumerable today. The AST firewalls (layersChecker via the TS type checker, checkCyclicDependencies over compiled out, propertyInitOrderChecker, tsec) + tsfmt/eslint/stylelint + telemetry extractor + the test matrix are all correct category-D non-goals. NOTE: the config's A5 indent_style 'C-tuning' worry (heavy filters.ts exemptions) is handled by include/exclude scoping, not a gap.

**Abstraction ideas.** A `shared_pin_matrix` / map-intersection variant of cross_file: relation:equals_on_shared_keys across a source manifest and N target manifests — for every key present in BOTH source and target, the values must be equal (the checkPackageJSON behavior, also hits Lerna/Nx/pnpm-workspace catalogs and any sub-package version-drift gate). This is the single highest-leverage new abstraction this group demonstrates: it is a true cross-file gap-narrowing (turns N hand-enumerated rules into one) and recurs across every multi-package JS/TS repo. Lower priority: an import_gate/file_graph forbidden_edges 'layer firewall from tsconfig.<layer>.json references' would approximate valid-layers-check WITHOUT the type checker for the cheap path-prefix cases, though the full check is genuinely type-directed (D).

*Notes.* vscode is the strongest case in this group for 'alint orchestrates, the type-checker firewalls stay'. The genuinely declarative wins (copyright, 2 version pacts, no-extensionsGallery) are clean; the only stretch (full shared-dep map) is enumerable today and a clean future-sugar target.
